Is it ok for us to remove automatic payments to allow you to make manual payments? Once you have paid the account you will need to set up automatic payments again via My Optus app\"\n= Credit applied - Balance already paid via automatic payment: \"The credit will be applied to your account within 48 hours. The credit will come off your next automatic payment. Note. You won't see the credit when checking your next estimated payment in My Optus app / My Account.
